What each line does: - `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` / `MAX_THINKING_TOKENS` — cap how much any single response (and its reasoning) can generate. Stops an agent from writing a 3,000-line essay per turn. Subagents inherit these. - `BASH_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` / `BASH_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` — kill a shell command after 3 min (hard ceiling 10 min). A hung build or a test that waits on stdin forever gets terminated instead of burning wall-clock and tokens on retries. - `deny` — belt-and-suspenders: agents physically cannot push or publish, so a runaway can't do outward-facing damage even if it tries.
